Impossible numeric values (technical noise)\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        idx = int(row['orig_idx'])\n        age = row['age']\n        bmi = row['bmi']\n        avg = row['avg_glucose_level']\n        if pd.isna(age) or pd.isna(bmi) or pd.isna(avg):\n            outlier_set.add(idx)\n            continue\n        if age > 120:\n            outlier_set.add(idx)\n        if bmi < 10 or bmi > 60:\n            outlier_set.add(idx)\n        if avg < 20 or avg > 800:\n            outlier_set.add(idx)\n\n    # 2. 3*IQR\n    for col in ['age', 'bmi', 'avg_glucose_level']:\n        s = df[col]\n        q1 = s.quantile(0.25)\n        q3 = s.quantile(0.75)\n        iqr = q3 - q1\n        if iqr == 0:\n            continue\n        lo = q1 - 3 * iqr\n        hi = q3 + 3 * iqr\n        mask = (s < lo) | (s > hi)\n        for _, row in df[mask].iterrows():\n            idx = int(row['orig_idx'])\n            if col == 'avg_glucose_level':\n                val = row['avg_glucose_level']\n                same = df[df['avg_glucose_level'] == val]\n                total = len(same)\n                sc = (same['stroke'] == 1).sum()\n                pct = sc / total if total > 0 else 0\n                if pct >= 0.5:\n                    continue  # not outlier\n                outlier_set.add(idx)\n            else:\n                outlier_set.add(idx)\n\n    # 3 & 4.
